7～9 月期実質GDP は年率＋1.0％と 
プラス成長を予想（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比＋0.2％（年率＋1.0％）と、4～6月期（前期比＋
0.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半期連続でのプラス成長を予想 
○ 個人消費が伸び悩む一方、設備投資や民間在庫が押し上げる見込み。なお、公的在庫と輸入では
中東情勢を主因とする一時的な変動が生じ、成長率にプラス・マイナス両面で影響 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）はプラス成長 を
予想 
11月16日 （月） に、 内閣府から2026年7～9月期の 「四半期別GDP速 報（QE）」
（1次速報値）が公表される。現時点で入手可能な基礎統計の結果を踏まえ
ると、7～9月期の実質GDPは前期比＋0.2％（ 年 率＋1.0％） となる見込みだ。
4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半
期連続でプラス成長になると予想する（図表）。 
7～9月期は、内需の柱である個人消費が伸び悩む一方、 設備投資の拡大や、
製造業を中心とする在庫積み増しが成長率を押し上げるとみられる。その他
では、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要因として現れる
が、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイナス要因になるなど、 
図表 2026 年7～9 月期GDP（1 次速報）予測

前期比に対する寄与度（％Pt）

### Page 2

2

中東情勢による一時的な変動も引き続き成長率に影響する見込みだ。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
個人消費は伸び悩み 。耐
久財の特需剥落や、 根強
い節約志向が影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％（4～6月期：同＋0.0％） と、2四半期連続で伸び
悩む結果になろう。消費の弱さの背景には、 複合的な要因があると考えられ
る。 
耐久財では、自動車とエアコンの「特需」剥落が押し下げ要因になろう。
自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に廃止されたこと
を受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていたが、8月以降は再
び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年4月の省エネ基準
引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要が生じていたが、
景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入った後は一服した
模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減少もエアコンなど
季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
食品やエネルギー、 各種消耗品などの非耐久財では、物価高を背景とする
根強い節約志向により、 弱含みが続いているようだ。今回のインフレ局面が
本格化する前の2020年と比較すると、非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6
年半でおよそ1.3倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上
昇が、 消費者の体感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、 購入数量の抑制
につながっている可能性がある。 
サービス消費は、 イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、 サービスや価格帯、 顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。 また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・ 宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、 レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投 資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
設備投資は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.9％） と、3四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスへ転じると予想する。 
直近の設備投資関連指標をみると、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財
総供給指数（除く輸送機械）が7～8月に大きく上昇した。また第3次産業活
動指数では、ソフトウェア業の実質売上高が同じく7～8月に増加したことが
確認でき、どちらも7～9月期の設備投資が拡大したことを示唆している。 
加えて、前期の4～6月期に発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特許権の海外
譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少） がなくなったこともプラス要因だ。た
だし、 これは裏を返せば、7～9月期のQEにおける設備投資の伸びが実態以上
に強い数字になるということでもあり、評価する際には留意が必要である。 
住宅投資は 前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.8％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.6％ ）と、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスとなろう。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～8月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上

### Page 3

3

昇による需要低迷に加えて、 足元では地震や台風などによる着工 ・ 進捗の遅
れが発生している可能性がある。 
民間 在庫は 2四半期連続
で成長率にプラス寄与 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、＋0.2％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラスが続く見込みだ。AI関連需要の強さなどを背景に、製
造業で在庫積み増しの動きが進んでいることが影響すると考えられる。な
お、4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を
押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足
が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響は一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要 は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラスに 
公的需要は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.8％）と、2四半期ぶりの前期
比プラスを予想する。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じるとみられることだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変
動の寄与度が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備
蓄の減少が小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公
的在庫の減少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.3％Pt程度のプラ
ス寄与になると見込まれる。 
その他の公的需要項目では、政府消費を前期比＋0.2％（4～6月期：同＋
1.7％）と予想する。4～6月期は高校授業料・小学校給食費の無償化開始に
より高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした特殊要因が剥落し、緩やかな
増加ペースに戻ると考えられる。公共投資は前期比▲0.5％（4～6月期：同
▲0.5％）と、土木関連工事の減少を受けて2四半期連続のマイナスを見込む。 
輸出が小幅に減少する 一
方、 輸入が増加し、外需は
4四半期ぶりのマイナス
寄与 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.3％（4～6月期：同＋0.4％）と小幅に減少す
る一方、輸 入が同＋1.3％（4～6月 期：同▲1.7％）と増加に転じる見込みだ。
実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.3％Ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナ
スが予想される。 
輸入の増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化により4
～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホルム
ズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの輸入
量が確保されている。 
輸入はGDPの控除項目であることから、7～9月期は輸入増加が成長率の下
押し要因になる。 ただし、代替調達の進展に伴って国家備蓄原油の放出が抑
制され、 先述したように7～9月期は公的在庫変動が成長率にプラス寄与する
とみられることから、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果の一部は公的在庫変
動により相殺されるだろう。 
輸出では、前期の4～6月期に特許権の海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げ
た （これは4～6月期の設備投資の押し下げ要因でもある） 反動が現れ、 一時
的に下振れするだろう。一方、旺盛なAI関連需要が支えになり、半導体・電

### Page 4

4

子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸出は堅調に推移する見込みだ。インバウ
ンド需要 （非居住者家計の国内での直接購入） は、 日中対立の継続による中
国人訪日客の少なさに加え、 航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の伸び悩みに
より、低調な結果になると見込まれる。 
2026 年度後半 の実質 GDP
は小幅プラス と予想 。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期から減速するものの、4四半期
連続でプラス成長になると予想する。GDPの約半分を占める個人消費の伸び
悩みが足を引っ張っているが、設備投資や製造業を中心とする在庫積み増し
がけん引する格好だ。 
先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～2027年3月）の 実質GDP
は、 均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。個人消費が振るわな
い一方で、 設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろう。2026年度全体の
実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平均水準が押し上げら
れることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％程度とまずまずの
成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。 原油 ・ ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、 コア消費者物価指数 （生鮮食品を除く総合） は年度末にかけて前年比＋
3％程度まで上昇すると予想される（ただし、2026年末から政府の電気・ガ
ス料金支援策が実施されれば、0.4～0.5％Pt程度の押し下げ要因になる可能
性がある）。加えて、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを
中心とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、 一時的に消費が落ち込む可能
性がある。 
他方で、年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調
査） では、2026年度の設備投資計画 （ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投
資額 （除く土地投資額）） が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した （6月 調 査：
同＋8.8％）。生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、
設備の不足感が強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、 設備投資は今後拡大
傾向で推移していく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」 （特定生産性向上
設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、 日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、 企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、 また、 投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、 中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。

### Page 5

5

日銀は10月会合で 政策金
利を据え置き 。次回利上
げは12月と予想 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、 日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％） は、次回12
月17・18日の会合と予想する。 前回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて 「3カ月
に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、 日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の 「半年強
に1回」から加速していることを印象付けるものになろう。 
企業の価格転嫁行動は一段と積極化しており、植田総裁が9月会合後の記
者会見で 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明したように、日本銀行は「物価引
き上げ」 から 「物価上振れ抑制」 へと金融政策運営の舵を切っている。12月
の追加利上げ後も、 金利上昇の影響を確認しながら、 日銀は最終的に2027年
半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％まで引き上げていくだろう。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。 
（https://www.mizuhobank.co.jp/corporate/mhri/research/report/pdf/outlook261002.pdf）

Document 058: 2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30

Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 10 月30 日

1/3 
2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測（1 次速報） 
～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

小幅ながら４四半期連続のプラス成長を予想、景気回復基調は維持 
11 月16 日に公表される2026 年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋
0.1％）と予測する。４四半期連続のプラス成長になるだろう1。 
物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
一方、設備投資が高い伸びになることが下支えになる見込みである。設備投資の伸びには特殊要
因も一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。個人消費が弱含むなかでも
プラス成長を維持するとみられることは、企業部門の底堅さが景気を下支えしていることを示し
ている。過去３四半期に比較的高い成長が続いた後であることも踏まえれば、今回の減速をもっ
て景気の基調が弱まったと判断する必要はないだろう。家計部門の弱さは懸念材料だが、企業部
門の底堅さは維持されており、景気は引き続き緩やかな回復基調にあると評価できる。

輸入増による成長率押し下げと公的在庫のプラス寄与は表裏一体 
７-９月期のＧＤＰを評価する際には、原油輸入と公的在庫の動きをセットで捉える必要があ
る。４-６月期は、中東情勢の悪化によって原油輸入が激減したことが、ＧＤＰ統計上は輸入減を
通じて成長率を押し上げた。一方、輸入の減少による国内供給の不足を補うために国家備蓄原油
が放出され、公的在庫の取り崩しが進んだ。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.5％Pt
（前期比年率寄与度▲2.1％Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し下げた。輸入減による成長率の押し上げ
は、公的在庫の減少と表裏一体の関係にあった。 
７-９月期は、これと逆の動きが生じるとみられる。政府・民間による代替調達の進展によって
原油輸入が回復するため、統計上は輸入増が成長率の押し下げ要因になる。一方、輸入の回復に
伴って国家備蓄原油の取り崩しは一服しており、公的在庫の減少幅は前期に比べて大きく縮小す
るとみられる。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.4％Pt（前期比年率寄与度＋1.5％
Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し上げることが予想される。

1 10 月 30 日時点で入手可能な経済指標を元に作成している。国際収支や家計調査等、今後公表される経済指標の結果
を踏まえて予測値を修正する可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/3 
輸入増による成長率の押し下げと公的在庫変動による押し上げは、ともに原油の供給環境が改
善したことに伴う動きである。両者を個別に景気の強弱として評価することは適切ではなく、一
体として見るべきだろう。 
むしろ重要なのは、代替調達の進展によって国家備蓄への依存を減らしながら必要な原油を確
保できる状況になり、資源不足が生産活動を大きく制約するリスクが低下していることである。
ＧＤＰ統計上、輸入の増加は成長率の押し下げ要因となるものの、経済実態としては国内で利用
可能な資源の供給回復を意味している。この点は、７-９月期の日本経済を評価するうえで、むし
ろ前向きに捉えるべきだろう。

需要項目別の動向 
個人消費は前期比▲0.2％と小幅減少を予想する。８～９月に各地で相次いだ大雨や台風などの
天候不順が外出機会やサービス消費を抑制した可能性がある。また、４-６月期には自動車やエア
コンなどの耐久財販売が増加したが、７-９月期にはこうした一時的な押し上げ効果が剥落した。
加えて、物価高による節約志向が根強いなか、食料品や日用品などの非耐久財消費にも弱さがみ
られる。高い賃上げや底堅い雇用環境は下支え要因となっているものの、天候不順という一時的
な要因もあって個人消費は総じて力強さを欠く結果になったとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と、高い伸びを予想する。４-６月期には特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研
究開発投資の減少という特殊要因があったが、７-９月期はその影響が剥落することが押し上げ要
因になった面はある。もっとも、こうした特殊要因を除いても設備投資は明確に増加したと考え
られ、底堅い動きとなっている。企業収益が高水準を維持するなか、人手不足に対応した省力化
投資やデジタル・ＡＩ関連投資への需要は根強く、企業の設備投資意欲は引き続き強い。設備投
資計画も高い伸びとなっており、設備投資は均してみれば緩やかな増加基調にあると判断してよ
いだろう。 
なお、特許権の海外への譲渡により、テクニカルに４-６月期はサービス輸出が上振れ、設備投
資が下振れたが、７-９月期はその剥落によりサービス輸出が下振れ、設備投資が上振れとなる見
込みである。両者は相殺し合うため、成長率全体でみれば概ねニュートラルとなる。 
輸出は前期比＋1.2％と４四半期連続の増加を予想する。４-６月期に大口要因によって研究開
発サービスの受取が急増した分が剥落することで、サービス輸出が減少する一方、財輸出が高い
伸びとなり、財貨・サービス輸出全体では増加を維持したとみられる。半導体製造装置などの資
本財輸出が好調だった。地域別では、ＥＵ向けが低調だった一方、ＡＳＥＡＮ、ＮＩＥｓ向けの
資本財輸出が伸びている。また、４-６月期に急減した中東向け輸出が持ち直したことも押し上げ
につながっている。 
輸入は前期比＋3.3％と大幅な増加が予想される。４-６月期には資源輸入の激減により輸入が
大幅に減少していたが、代替調達の進展により７-９月期は明確に持ち直した。この結果、外需寄
与度は前期比▲0.3％Pt（前期比年率▲1.3％Pt）になったと予想される。 
ただし、この外需寄与度のマイナスは、資源輸入の回復に伴う輸入の増加によって生じるもの

Summary: Our first take on Unimicron’s 3Q26 results (released on 27 October) is that 
earnings were better than expected, driven by stronger-than-expected non-operating 
gains. We attribute the upside primarily to a smaller-than-feared valuation loss on UMC in 
3Q26. In addition, the company mentioned that other investments, including Unimicron 
shares held through investment vehicles, contributed c.TWD3bn of mark-to-market gains in 
3Q26. As for the core business, 3Q26 revenue reached TWD52.7bn (up 23% QoQ, up 
55% YoY), with gross margin expanding 4.0ppt QoQ to 28.8% and operating margin rising 
4.5ppt QoQ to 20.0%, supported by improving profitability in ABF substrates and HDI. We 
believe the profitability performance was broadly in line with the latest buy-side 
expectations. Looking ahead, while the company does not provide numerical guidance, 
revenue and gross margin should continue to improve in 4Q26. Given Unimicron’s leading 
position across most CSP AI ASIC projects, Nvidia GPUs, and EMIB-T, we believe the 
company should continue to benefit from a favourable pricing environment amid the 
current supply-demand dynamics and ongoing specification upgrades, including larger 
substrate body sizes and technology migration. We have a Buy (1) rating on Unimicron 
with a 12M TP of TWD1,500, based on a target PER of 35x applied to our 1-year-forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand.

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on Unimicron’s 3Q26 results (released on 27 October). We will 
review our model in due course.

• 3Q26 results beat expectations. Unimicron’s 3Q26 earnings came in at TWD10.1bn 
(EPS: TWD6.15), beating our estimate of TWD4.2bn and Bloomberg consensus of 
TWD6.7bn by 140% and 49%, respectively, mainly due to higher-than-expected non-
operating gains. In our previous note, Unimicron recognised c.TWD8bn of mark-to-
market gains on UMC (2303 TT, TWD122.5, Neutral [3], covered by Rick Hsu) in 2Q26. 
We had originally expected Unimicron to recognise c.TWD5bn of valuation losses in 
3Q26, given UMC’s share-price performance in July. However, the actual valuation loss 
of c.TWD0.7bn was substantially better than feared. In addition, the company attributed 
part of the strength to c.TWD3bn of other mark-to-market gains, including gains related 
to Unimicron shares held through its investment vehicles. This is broadly consistent with 
the reported 3Q26 total non-operating contribution of c.TWD2.6bn after accounting for 
the UMC valuation loss and other non-operating items.

Unimicron Technology Corp (3037 TT) 
27 October 2026 
2

• Looking at the core business, 3Q26 revenue reached TWD52.7bn (up 23% QoQ, up 
55% YoY), with gross margin expanding 4.0ppt QoQ to 28.8% and operating margin 
rising 4.5ppt QoQ to 20.0%, supported by improving profitability in ABF substrates and 
HDI. With stronger operating leverage, operating profit surged 58% QoQ to 
TWD10.53bn. We believe the gross-margin and operating-margin performance was 
broadly in line with recent buy-side expectations for modestly improving profitability in 
ABF substrates and HDI, given that ABF margin was already high at c.40% in 2Q26, 
alongside a higher revenue contribution from a large end customer. We estimate ABF 
substrate margin at c.42-44% in 3Q26 (vs. c.40% in 2Q26).

• Business outlook. While the company does not provide numerical guidance, revenue 
and gross margin should continue to improve in 4Q26 amid the current substrate supply 
shortage. In addition, the company is aggressively expanding capacity at its Guangfu 
and Yangmei plants over 2027-29, with roughly c.30% annual ABF capacity expansion, 
which should become a key growth driver for Unimicron. Given Unimicron’s leading 
position across most CSP AI ASIC projects, Nvidia GPUs, and EMIB-T, we believe the 
company should continue to benefit from a favourable pricing environment amid the 
current supply-demand dynamics and ongoing specification upgrades, including larger 
substrate body sizes and technology migration.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Unimicron with a 12M TP of TWD1,500, based on a target 
PER of 35x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: less-than-
expected demand.

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)

Stable traditional businesses and EMS outlook — Traditional packaging and test
demand is improving, supported by utilization gains and a recovery in automotive
and industrial, as well as AI-adjacent chips. Management expects general-market
business to grow again in 2027, despite softer smartphone and PC demand. For
2027, ASEH expects further EMS growth, led by high-performance computing, and

is pursuing system-level solutions that combine EMS capabilities with ATM
technologies.
LEAP upside intact; GM upward trend structural
Following the solid 3Q26 result, it expects 4Q26 consolidated revenue to grow 6–
8% QoQ, with gross margin of 23–24% and operating margin of 14.5–15.5%. ATM
revenue is forecast to increase 11–13% QoQ, with GM improving 100–200 bps. EMS
revenue is expected to be flat QoQ, with an operating margin of about 2%.
Looking into 2027, management expects LEAP revenue to double, with further GM

improvement as LEAP ramps and utilization rises across traditional packaging and
test. Demand remains ahead of available capacity: assembly is expected to grow
faster than test, with investment prioritizing assembly and wafer probing. Full-
process revenue is projected to exceed US$1bn in 2027, while CPO is expected to
contribute more than US$500m. Capacity and facility space remain key
constraints; ASE plans to increase capex to US$11.5bn in 2027 given its raised

2026 outlook. It also expects traditional-market growth to continue, supported by
automotive, industrial and AI-adjacent demand, despite softer smartphone and PC
markets.
On the back of its structural GM upside and more LEAP revenue contribution, we
lift our 2026/2027 /2028 earnings projection by 12%/18%/16%. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$908, based on an unchanged 25x 2027 EPS.

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
8
ASE Technology Holding
Company description
Advanced Semiconductor Engineering (ASE), incorporated in 1984 and listed
at the TWSE in 1989, is one of the world's leading companies in the
semiconductor packaging and testing sector. In June 2016, ASE entered into a
Joint Share Exchange Agreement with SPIL under which ASE Industrial was
eventually to be formed as a holding company with 100% equity interests in

both ASE and SPIL. ASE Industrial was listed on Taiwan's main board on April
30, 2018, the same day on which ASE and SPIL were delisted.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on ASE Holding. The combined ASE-SPIL entity is the
highest-quality OSAT worldwide, in our view, with its market share
leadership, superior operations, economies of scale, and customer/product
diversification. Conditions imposed by the Chinese government on the ASE-
SPIL merger, which required them to operate independently, were lifted in

2020. Consequently, we believe ASE Holding will benefit from further revenue
and operating synergies as well as the trend for advanced packaging.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$908 is based on 25x 2027 EPS average. Our PER is
higher than its five-year range as we expect ASEH to see accelerating
earnings growth in the coming years with GM at upward expansion trend,
mainly driven by its promising outlook in LEAP business and strong expansion
for long-term AI demand. At our target price, the shares would trade at a

2026/27E P/B of 10x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent ASE shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) poor efficiency and less synergy created from merger with
SPIL; 2) failure to gain market share in advanced packaging; 3) industry
oversupply due to aggressive capacity expansion; 4) severe pricing erosion
due to competition; and 5) a longer-than-expected semiconductor inventory
correction.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

Yageo
Company description
Established in 1977 and listed in 1993, Yageo is a leading supplier of passive
electronic components based in Taiwan. The company offers a wide array of
products including MLCC, chip resistors, magnetics, tantalum, and other
components, aiming to be the total solution provider for its customers. By
capacity, the company currently ranks No. 1 in chip resistor and tantalum and

No. 3 in MLCC and inductor globally. Key customers of Yageo include leading
EMS, ODMs, and distributors. The company also has 29 sales offices, 51
manufacturing sites, and 20 R&D centers in 29 nations worldwide.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Yageo shares. Yageo is widely known as a passive
electronic component supplier. Although passive components are generally
viewed as a commodity, Yageo is no longer a commodity play, in our view,

given the company’s foray into premium markets through M&As, which
should help mitigate the impact of sector cyclicality and order volatility. We
expect a more diversified product portfolio to drive stable earnings and better
margins, while improving utilization in its commodity component business
following the end of inventory digestions should add additional fuel.
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,000, based on 23x average 2027E-28E
earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the company’s earnings

performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market.
Our target multiple of 23x is at the higher end of the trading range since 2022
but in line with global peers average. During this period the company has gone
through the cycles, successfully integrated the acquired business to enhance
its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

3481 群創光電(INNOLUX)

壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收 637 億，毛利率 14.6% ，營利率 2.6% ，EPS 0.57
元。受惠於非顯示器比重持續提升 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。  
 公司今年處分三座廠房，累計獲利約為 200 億元，目前資產處
分已告一段落 ， 暫無進一步出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP
投資調整既有廠區資源。  
 因上半年提前拉貨以及零組件漲價影響，消費型顯示器需求動
能相對保守；半導體事業部分， Chip-first 製程已進入量產，
每月出貨量約 4,000 萬片，而 RDL-first 與 TGV （玻璃基板）
製程部分，目前正與客戶緊密合作推進，預期玻璃基板封裝產
品將於 2028 年推出。

一、 2Q26 毛利率達 14.6% ，EPS 0.57 元 
群創 2Q26 營收 637 億，QoQ -4.6% ，YoY +13.1% ， 而獲利方面，
2Q26 營業毛利 92.7 億， QoQ -3.6% ，YoY +96.8% ，毛利率
14.6% ，QoQ +0.1ppts ，YoY +6.2ppts ，營業淨利 16.3 億，營利
率 2.6% ，2Q26 業外淨利 16.3 億，稅後淨利 46.5 億，單季稅後
EPS 0.57 元。而就上半年來看， 1H26 消費型顯示器佔整體營收
45% ，平均毛利率為 6%至 10% ；商用顯示器佔 13% ，平均毛利
率 16% 至 20%； 非顯示器領域佔42%， 平均毛利率11% 至 15% ，
受惠於商用、非顯示器比重提升， 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。

二、資產處分告一段落，後續聚焦 FOPLP 產能  
為充實營運資金、活化公司旗下資產，群創今年處分三座廠房， 3
月將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59 億元 )，以及
將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )。公司 4 月再
度宣布，將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價 148.5 億元售予日月光
投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年群創認列的售廠總
獲利約為 197.59 億元。公司表示，目前資產處分已告一段落，暫
無進一步大規模出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP 投資調整既有
廠區資源。

三. 消費性面板短期保守，持續朝高階封裝領域布局  
公司公告 9 月營收 199.95 億， MoM +6.9% ，YoY +0.7% ，3Q26
營收 575.8 億， QoQ -9.4% ，YoY -0.4% ，今年累計營收 1879.3
億， YoY +10.6% 。展望後市，在面板業務方面，受記憶體等零組
投資評等：區間操作 
目標價位：42.8~54.6 元 
     1.45x-1.85x27BVPS

全年營收 2659 億元，YoY +8.5% ，毛利率 14.3% ，營利率 3.1% ，
稅後淨利 892 億，全年稅後 EPS 1.12 元，每股淨值 29.51 元。

四. 投資建議為 42.8~54.6 元區間操作  
公司持續投入半導體 FOPLP 領域，聚焦轉型、資產活化題材，但
考量到 4Q26 至明年上半年面板事業展望保守 ， 半導體事業部分，
後續仍須持續觀察客戶認證、量產進度及營運貢獻狀況。目前股
價交易於 2027 BVPS 約 1.64 倍 PBR 水準，以公司過往 PBR 介
於 0.4x~2.5x 區間水準，給予區間操作投資建議，目標價為
42.8~54.6 元(1.45x~1.85 x 2027 BVPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
群創光電 (INNOLUX) 為全球第三大面板廠，主要產品為大尺寸 (大
於 9 吋)與中小尺寸 (9 吋以下 )與延伸技術之薄膜電晶體液晶顯示
器(TFT-LCD) 相關產品，大尺寸產品應用於液晶電視、公共看板、
桌上型監視器、筆記型電腦與工業控制等；中小尺寸則為平板電
腦、可攜式影音播放器、車載、航空船舶、智能家居及手機、穿
戴裝置等應用，也可選擇性搭配各類型觸控面板；另針對特殊使
用需求，也提供應用於醫療、軍用、教育與電子紙應用之產品。
集團總部位於苗栗縣竹南鎮，台灣生產基地位於竹南、南科、台
南、高雄等地，另有海外生產基地則包含中國深圳、中國寧波、
中國南京、中國南海、荷蘭等地。 2025 年公司營收占比大宗消費
品(Commodity) 55% ；非大宗消費品 (Non-commodity) 16% ；非
顯示器領域群 (Non-display) 29% 。公司 NB 客戶為 DELL，液晶電
視客戶包括 Panasonic 、Sharp 、Sony、Toshiba 、Samsung 、TCL
等；手機及平板電腦客戶為 Samsung 、Amazon 、Apple 及華為；
車用面板客戶包括 BMW、福 特、General Motors 、 特斯拉等車廠。
國內主要競爭廠商為友達光電、瀚宇彩晶，國外廠商則包括

2. 群創連售三廠房，預估獲利近 200 億元 
為充實營運資金並挹注未來發展動能，群創近期積極活化旗下資
產。繼 3 月先後將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59
億元 )，以及將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )
後，群 創 於15 日再度宣布 ， 將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價148.5
億元售予日月光投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年以
來，預估公司可望認列售廠總獲利達 197.59 億元。

4 
二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 需求推動，FOPLP 與玻璃基板市場 2030 年將突破 80 億美元 
根據  Counterpoint Research 報告，受惠於 AI 與高效能運算
(HPC) 強勁需求 ， 全球扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP) 與玻璃基板市場
規模，預計將從 2024 年的 6.5 億美元飆升至 2030 年的 81 億美
元以上，其中 AI 與 HPC 應用將佔 FOPLP 總營收的 45.6% 。相較
於傳統有機基板，玻璃基板具備高互連密度、尺寸穩定與抗翹曲
等優勢，能支援次世代小晶片 (Chiplet) 架構與大型 AI 處理器，吸
引台積電、 Intel 、三星電機、日月光及力成等大廠積極擴展產能
與研發。區域佈局上，預計 2030 年台灣、日本與中國大陸將囊括
全球 84.8% 的面板級封裝產能。儘管目前仍面臨面板尺寸標準化
與玻璃穿孔 (TGV) 製程等量產挑戰，但在產業鏈合作推動下，整體
市場前景依舊十分樂觀。

圖三、 2024-2030 年各地區 FOPLP 面板產能預估

資料來源： Counterpoint

2. 零組件成本壓力與需求衰退交織，10 月面板價格全面持平 
根據 TrendForce 預估，進入第 4 季後，受上游零組件成本上漲壓
力遽增及終端採購需求衰退雙重影響， 10 月 TV、MNT 與 NB 三
大面板價格預估將全面持平。 TV 面板部分，雖有原廠調控產能並
喊漲，但主要品牌第 4 季採購需求季減約 5%，短期難接受漲價；
而 MNT 面板需求持續減弱，惟成本壓力使面板廠無讓步空間，買
賣雙方對持平具共識，後續將視 TV 面板能否成功漲價而定；另
外， NB 面板則因前六大品牌第 4 季採購需求恐季減近 30% ，出
貨動能嚴重萎縮，在成本高漲且降價無助爭取訂單的情況下，雙
方亦達成維持價格穩定的共識。

前 Chip-first 製程已進入量產，每月出貨量突破 4,000 萬片，預計
今年與 2027 年將維持雙位數年增率 ， 至於RDL-first 與 TGV 製程
部分，由於 TGV 玻璃基板具備優異的散熱效率、低翹曲以及高密
度互連優勢，將瞄準 AI 與 HPC 晶片需求，目前正與全球策略夥
伴及客戶緊密合作推進 ， 預期玻璃基板封裝產品將於2028 年前推
出。預 估2026 全年營收 2451 億元 ，YoY +8.1% ， 毛利率14.2% ，
營利率 1.9%， 稅後淨利805 億 ， 全年稅後EPS 1.01 元。預 估2027

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

### Page 1

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 건설/원전 | 2026. 10. 30  
숫자로 증명하는 중

3Q26 Review: 에너빌부문 믹스개선, 두산밥캣 호실적 
두산에너빌리티 3분기 매출액은 4조 4,494억원으로 전년동기대비 14.7% 증가, 컨센
서스에 부합했다. 영업이익은 2,612억원으로 전년동기대비 90.6% 증가, 컨센서스 영
업이익 2,464억원 대비 6.0% 상회하는 호실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티부문의 원자
력, 가스터빈 매출 증가에 따른 믹스 개선 효과와 두산밥캣의 가격정책 효과에 힘입은 
마진 개선으로 안정적인 실적 기조가 이어지고 있어 긍정적이다. 분기 환평가손실로 
인해 순이익은 여타 수출기업과 유사하게 컨센서스 대비 하회했다. 에너빌부문의 수주
는 누계 10조원으로 목표대비 75%를 채웠으며, 수주 대기중인 안건들을 고려할 때 
당분간 수주 업사이클이 몇년간 지속될 것임이 분명한 시점이다. 
기회가 아닌 경쟁력, 모멘텀이 아닌 수주  
두산에너빌리티에 대해 투자의견 Buy 유지하나, 목표주가를 95,000원으로 하향했다. 
목표 EBITDA는 2035년 EBITDA에 WACC 8%를 적용, 목표 EV/EBITDA는 Global 
Peers인 GE Vernova의 2026년 EV/EBITDA 멀티플에서 자회사 실적 변동성을 고
려해 10% 할인 적용했다. 호실적과 수주 증가에도 GEV의 멀티플 감소와 순차입금 
증가로 인한 NAV 감소를 밸류에이션에 반영한 결과이다. 전일 주가는 한수원의 체코 
원전 시공사 협상 결렬로 인해 7.65% 하락 마감했다. 시공 수주는 아쉽지만 두산에너
빌리티의 체코 원전 기자재 납품에는 전혀 영향이 없으며, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가로 경
제적해자인 대형 가스 터빈 수급 쇼티지는 당분간 지속될 전망임을 고려할 때 전일 주
가는 낙폭 과대로 판단한다. 1) 두산에너빌리티의 가스터빈은 이미 2031년 출하 물량
에 대한 예약 주문이 마무리되고 있으며, 2) 또한 AP1000의 우량한 수주 파이프라인
과, 3) 뉴스케일파워, 테라파워, 롤스로이스, X Energy 등의 SMR 제작을 위한 협업 역
시 지속되고 있다는 점에 주목한다. 전일 기사를 통해 뉴스케일파워 SMR 1호 제작 계
약을 체결했다는 소식을 전했으며, 이제 에너지 트렌지션은 기회가 아닌 회사의 경쟁
력으로, 가스터빈과 원전은 모멘텀이 아니라 수주로 입증되고 있다.  
 Analyst 김세련 
sally.kim@ls-sec.co.kr

목표주가 2,000,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.02) 
1,354,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

(단위: 십억원, %)

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속원-_fba73583.pdf

### Page 1

2026.09.21   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다.

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

Document 262: Business Checkpoint_ Looking into the logistics Profitability growth was positive in the third quarter, but logistics costs and supply chain pressures are creating challenges. 2 mi

Source PDF: all report pdf/Business Checkpoint_ Looking into the logistics Profitability growth was positive in the third quarter, but logistics costs and supply chain pressures are creating challenges. 2 min read.pdf

### Page 1

13028181     1

Economy 
Business Checkpoint: Looking into the logistics 
15 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Small and mid-sized businesses entered Q3 in relatively solid financial condition, with profitability growth up across both 
groups, according to Bank of America business account data. Nearly half of estimated tariff payments had been refunded by 
September, though the share of these refunds relative to revenue has peaked. 
• In Bank of America business payments data, logistics spending growth per client was up four times as much for small than mid-
sized firms. Additionally, distribution and inventory transactions growth per client has outpaced spending since the spring of 
2025. 
• Growing transportation bottlenecks are re-emerging ahead of the holiday season. Trucking capacity has tightened as driver 
availability remains constrained, according to BofA Global Research. This has pushed freight rates higher and driven strong 
transportation sector payroll growth, particularly among mid-sized businesses, according to Bank of America payments data.

Business Checkpoint is a new, regular publication from Bank of America Institute. It provides a real-time view of the financial health, opportunities 
and challenges facing small and mid-sized businesses across the US economy, leveraging the depth and breadth of Bank of America's proprietary 
data. By analyzing activity among both small and mid-sized firms, Business Checkpoint offers timely insights into business profitability, hiring, 
investment and spending trends. Such data is not intended to be reflective or indicative of, and should not be relied upon as, the results of operations, 
financial condition or performance of Bank of America. 
Businesses end Q3 in fair shape 
While profitability growth remained positive for both small and mid-sized firms in the third quarter, small firms outpaced their 
mid-sized counterparts for the second consecutive quarter (Exhibit 2). Still, there’s been a notable reversal in the profitability of 
mid-sized businesses, growing this quarter after declining slightly for the past two.  
The continued rollout of tariff refunds is serving as a tailwind to some firms' cash positions. That is particularly true for small 
firms. According to Bank of America account data, by September approximately 47% of estimated International Emergency 
Economic Powers Act-related tariff payments had been refunded to companies with less than $50 million in annual revenue.  
Exhibit 1: Tariff refunds as a share of income for businesses <$50M 
in annual revenue peaked in June

According to Bank of America business payments data, the percentage point difference in tariff refunds as a share of revenue 
for firms with less than $50 million in annual revenue spiked in June but dropped to nearly zero in September. At the same time, 
the percentage point difference in tariff payments as a share of expenses began rising again relative to the 2023 average 
(Exhibit 1). This suggests that while tariffs continue to affect companies, the refund program has provided temporary relief for 
some businesses.  
Wholesale trade along with transportation and warehousing posted solid profitability growth across business size 
However, the benefits appear uneven. Such refunds have boosted cash flow, but they have not fully insulated firms from broader 
cost pressures.  
Looking across industries, mid-sized construction and wholesale firms had the strongest profitability growth in September 
(Exhibit 3). Notably, transportation and warehousing, as well as wholesale trade were among the few sectors showing positive 
profitability growth for both small and mid-sized firms last month. 
The persistence of logistics, freight and inventory-related cost pressures may help explain why profitability gains have remained 
relatively concentrated in transportation and wholesale trade. However, these sectors also have a higher exposure to rising gas 
prices within their margins (read more on this in the August Small Business Checkpoint). 
Exhibit 3: For both small and mid-sized businesses, profitability growth was positive in transportation and warehousing as well as wholesale trade 
in September 
Profitability growth by sector and business size in September (YoY%, six-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The cost of maintaining supply chains is rising 
In fact, the prices paid component of the September services reading from the Institute for Supply Management indicates still-
building price pressure for firms (Exhibit 4). This marked the highest reading since July 2022.   
Using Bank of America internal data, we identified payments to logistics and freight companies to assess a key part of supply 
chain costs. In June, logistics and freight payments’ growth per client from small businesses grew nearly 30%, though has 
moderated to 12.7% YoY in September (Exhibit 5). Meanwhile, mid-sized firms’ payments growth in this category was up just 
3.2% YoY in September. 
The divergence suggests logistics costs may be disproportionately affecting smaller firms, who are typically less capable of 
absorbing rising costs than larger firms. Larger companies also often benefit from dedicated logistics contracts and greater 
purchasing power, while smaller ones may be more exposed to spot-market transportation costs and fluctuations in freight 
pricing.

Evidence of more moving parts in companies’ supply chains 
Yet there is increasing evidence that tariffs and geopolitics are adding complexity to small and mid-sized companies’ supply 
chains. 
In Bank of America payments data, average distribution and inventory transaction growth per business client has increased 
steadily since the spring of 2025 despite spending growth in this category falling in September (Exhibit 6). Rising transaction 
growth alongside softer spending growth suggests businesses may be engaging with more suppliers, shipments or inventory 
movements even as overall procurement outlays moderates.  
Furthermore, in May 2026, the Global Supply Chain Pressure Index reached its highest reading since the summer of 2022 and has 
continued to remain elevated into October (Exhibit 7). This further emphasizes that transportation bottlenecks, delays and 
procurement challenges are becoming more common globally. While current readings remain below the extreme levels observed 
during the pandemic, the recent move higher indicates supply chain normalization may be reversing. 
Exhibit 6: Average distribution and inventory transaction growth 
per business client has increased steadily since the spring of 2025

The trials of trucking  
As of October 1, BofA Global Research's proprietary bi-weekly Truckload Demand Indicator (see Methodology) remained below 
its historical growth signal (Exhibit 9) and the Truck Capacity Indicator, which reflects shippers' views of available capacity, 
tightened significantly, falling from 39.2 to 28.4 (Exhibit 8). This suggests shippers are seeing fewer trucks available relative to 
demand than in the previous survey. 
According to BofA Global Research, the current trucking upcycle is being driven by constrained supply rather than a significant 
improvement in freight demand. While any positive movement in demand is an important indicator as the industry moves into 
peak shipping season between late September and early December, tighter capacity appears to be the dominant force 
supporting freight rates. 
Exhibit 8: Shippers’ view of available capacity has fallen 
significantly as of October 1, 2026 
BofA Truckload Capacity Indicator by year (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 9: The Demand Indicator has been sub-60 for the past two 
months after 4.5 months above 
BofA Truckload Demand Indicator (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The reduction in capacity reflects several factors, including stricter commercial driver's license (CDL) and training requirements, 
increased compliance and enforcement measures, and other regulatory changes that have reduced the pool of qualified drivers. 
This has also likely driven some wage inflation within the industry. According to Bank of America payments data, transportation 
and warehousing payroll growth per client has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses (Exhibit 10).  
Having to pay higher wages suggests labor shortages remain a challenge in the sector. Firms may increasingly need to offer 
higher compensation to attract and retain workers as trucking capacity tightens. In turn, such capacity puts upward pressure on 
freight rates, particularly in the spot market where small businesses may be more impacted. 
Exhibit 10: Transportation and warehousing payroll growth has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses 
Payroll payments per client by industry and business size (monthly, YoY%, three-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data

Logistics payments tend to increase in the second half of the year 
September marks a key run-up to the holiday season (read more on signs of holiday spending in the October Consumer 
Checkpoint), with spending on ocean shipping, containers, and other transportation services steadily accelerating ahead of the 
peak shopping period.  
In fact, over the past two years, the month of November has seen ocean shipping/container spending volumes well above their 
2023-2025 monthly average (Exhibit 11). The seasonal pattern likely reflects inventory restocking and holiday-related shipping 
activity. Ocean shipping volumes typically accelerate during the fall months as businesses prepare for year-end demand. 
Exhibit 11: Ocean shipping/container spending volumes were well above their 2023-2025 monthly average in November 
Logistics and freight average payments by category (monthly, indexed, 100 = monthly 2023-2025 average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Distributors and inventory payments’ growth fell ahead of holiday season 
Changes in logistics and freight spending often provide an early signal of inventory trends, as distributors account for a large 
share of supply chain-related spending (Exhibit 12). 
The sharp moderation in distribution and inventory spending growth per client for both small and mid-sized businesses indicates 
that much of the large-scale inventory accumulation seen earlier in 2025 has already occurred (Exhibit 13). In other words, 
businesses appear to be replenishing inventories for seasonal demand rather than significantly expanding stockpiles as they did 
earlier in the year. 
Overall, businesses seem to be entering the holiday season in relatively stable financial condition, supported by tariff refunds 
and positive profitability growth. However, rising logistics costs, tighter trucking capacity and elevated supply chain pressures 
suggest operating conditions are becoming more challenging.

chain-related payments

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business and/or Business Banking clients with a deposit account or a 
Small Business credit card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of 
America per Business client data represents activity spending from active Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card and at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and are 
generally defined as under $5mm in annual sales revenue. Mid-sized businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America 
and are generally defined as $5mm-$50mm in annual sales revenue. 
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Revenue tiers are determined by the combination of following factors: 1) stated revenue on small businesses credit applications, 2) actual 
account inflow into Bank of America Deposit Accounts, and 3) third party revenue estimation.  
The alternative hiring indicator consists of payments from Bank of America business clients to business-focused hiring firms which include both 
direct deposits through Automated Clearing House (ACH) and payments via credit and debit cards. 
Supply chain-related spending is comprised of payments to logistics, freight, distribution, inventory, delivery service, and trucking companies. 
These are identified from merchant names and MCC codes. 
BofA Global Research’s trucking indicators are a bi-weekly diffusion-style survey of shipper demand expectations over the next 0–3 months, 
built from responses from surveyed shippers. 
Data regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting

continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.

October 23, 2026 
A client-facing version of this report is available at the Deutsche Bank Research 
Portal. The following Deutsche Bank Research analysts contributed to this report: 
Brett Ryan (econ), Binky Chadha and Parag Thatte (equity strategy), Benjamin 
Soff (data centers), Daniel Knauff (software), George Hill (healthcare), Omotayo 
Okusanya (REITs), Peter Abramowitz (office REITs), Mark DeVries (consumer and 
specialty finance), Nicole DeBlase (MI/EE), Corinne Blanchard (clean tech), 
Richa Harnain (transports), and Scott Deuschle (aerospace and defense).  
With the 2026 US midterm elections approaching, we look at key investor 
debates surrounding the event and legislative priorities of the next Congress. 
Despite the uncertainty that midterms can bring, the market appears fairly 
confident on expectations for the overall results. While a surprise Republican-
majority victory in both chambers is still possible, consensus is converging 
around current polling indicating Democrats are likely to flip the seats needed for 
a House majority and may win a Senate majority. Historically, an incumbent 
president has lost seats in 20 of the last 22 midterms. Investor expectations are 
in line with this. Polymarket, for example, is currently above 90% for Democrats 
gaining control of the House and above 60% for Democrats gaining control of the 
Senate.  
With this set of expectations, key investor debates instead focus on the 
legislative agenda for hot-ticket policy issues. Namely, these hinge on AI and 
data centers, affordability (affecting healthcare, housing, and education loans), 
and economic and fiscal policy. Debates also surround possible influence of the 
new Congress on the war in Iran and, by extension, energy prices. In this report, 
we cut through the noise on what the next Congress could reasonably advance 
from these agendas, what the impact would be on affected sectors, and how to 
address market uncertainty – and perceived certainty – into the elections. 
Historically, midterm elections have proven to be a check on administration 
policies. The number of seats lost in the midterms by the incumbent party is tied 
to the president’s approval rating going into midterms, which in turn tends to 
align with consumer confidence. President Trump’s ~38% approval rating, at a 
record low for his tenure, would suggest a little over a 45-seat loss for the 
Republican Party (in the House and Senate combined). That said, polling in the 
Senate races that will determine control of the chamber largely remains within 
the margin of error, and in 2024, Trump won in at least five of the seven core toss-
up states. Thus, the Senate remains a much closer call than the House race.

Whatever the outcome of the  midterms, the legislative agenda will 
still face constraints from the White House. It is all but impossible for either party 
to gain a veto -proof majority  in either chamber, minimizing the likelihood of a 
large shift in fiscal or regulatory policy. The most  immediately obvious impact of 
Democrats taking the Senate would be the ability to slow or block executive 
branch appointees, including at the Federal Reserve Board of Governors or the 
Supreme Court, should any members step down before their terms end.

### Page 3

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Without garnering a two-thirds majority in both chambers, Democrats lack a clear 
path to unwind the 2025 tax cuts or enact AI-related regulatory legislation – at 
least, not without President Trump’s explicit approval. With respect to the near -
term fiscal policy outlook, the most important period for investors may be the 
“lame duck” session, the period after the November 3 election and before the new 
Congress convenes on January 3. Recall that current federal government funding 
expires on December 11, and the Trump administration is asking for a nearly 60% 
increase in defense spending, the largest since the Korean War.  In addition, the 
debt limit will become binding again around the middle of next year. In short, the 
actions during the lame duck session could determine whether the new Congress 
is seated amid a government shutdown, a contentious battle over the federal 
budget, and the debt limit looming in the background.  
In terms of the market reaction to midterm  elections, the risk-reward profile has 
typically been favorable , with 21 of the last 23 midterm years  seeing positive 
returns in the fourth quarter, with an average gain of 7% in the S&P. The gains are 
notable even after adjusting for typical positive year-end seasonality.

Figure 3: S&P Returns and Midterms

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP

It w ould not be unusual for the market to go sideways as investors hedge 
potential volatility and uncertainty, as we have seen previously ahead of other 
well-telegraphed pivotal events. This has historically been followed by a rally as 
the results became clear and  uncertainty dissipated, regardless of the result 
itself. In the current context, if election polls and prediction market probabilities 
continue being lopsided, the market may not treat the election as an uncertain 
risk event going in. Uncertainty could still ramp up after, and the typical midterm 
election rally may be pushed out. It is notable that the one prominent exception 
to the midterm rallies was in 2018 , when the market sold off sharply after the 
elections as uncertainty spiked around trade war 1.0 as well as Fed policy.

### Page 4

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

We note that the midterm elections also coincide with the third quarter earnings 
season, during which we expect companies to deliver growth of 34%. For next 
year, we expect growth to be a still -robust 17% with risks to the upside. 
Eventually, earnings tend to be the anchor for equities and have been for several 
years despite numerous domestic political as well as geopolitical shocks. 
Across sectors, most have rallied around past midterms in keeping with the 
market, but there isn’t a consistent pattern of out - or underperformance . This 
suggests that for the initial conditions for each sector, specific policies and 
positioning played a bigger role.  For that reason, we turn to a closer analysis of 
key legislative and policy debates impacting exposed sectors.

### Page 5

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

AI and Data Centers  
AI and data centers have become a campaign talking point for candidates from 
both the Democratic and Republican parties. This has spurred investor questions 
on whether to expect a national regulatory framework constraining data center 
buildouts. Some of th e headlines surrounding this debate overestimate what 
Congress can  accomplish through national legislation compared with what is 
possible at a state level, particularly during a presidential administration 
supportive of AI and data center acceleration. Still, the industry is responding to 
a political backdrop that is increasingly scrutinizing AI and data centers.  
The current administration has prioritized advancing AI and digital infrastructure 
development. The White House ’s AI Action Plan , released July 2025, included a 
set of executive orders supporting the rapid buildout of AI data centers by 
reducing certain federal regulatory and permitting burdens. The administration 
did not preempt state laws on data center infrastructure development and 
federal permitting orders generally do not override state zoning and utility rules. 
In March 2026 , the White House announced the Ratepayer Protection Pledge, 
under which large data center operators would voluntarily commit to covering 
the cost to upgrade generation, transmission, substations, or other necessary 
infrastructure to service these developments.  The industry’s response to these 
proposals has been largely constructive. The Data Center Coalition  (DCC), an 
industry group representing major  hyperscalers and data center  owners and 
operators came out in support of the initial version of the Pledge, while arguing 
that large -load customers across all industries also should cover the costs to 
upgrade energy infrastructure. According to the DCC , “the data center 
industry remains committed to being responsible partners where we  operate, 
which includes  paying our full energy costs  and investing in local jobs and 
workforce development.” 
Congress voted in September 2026 to codify the principles present in the Pledge 
via the Ratepayer Protection Act. The bill, which only required states to “consider” 
these standards, passed overwhelmingly in the House (417-3), but failed to reach 
60 votes in the Senate (57 -43). Critics noted the bill stopped short of imposing 
mandatory nationwide rules. 
Looking forward, we expect a Congress that is more focused on consumer 
protection might look to codify a more stringent version of these rules, requiring 
large-load customers to bear the full incremental costs associated with 
generation, transmission, and distribution upgrades.  However, even if such a 
framework were passed into law, we believe the overall economic impact to large 
data center operators would be relatively modest, for two reasons. 
For one, the White House would maintain a veto over  bills that include language 
it views as overly burdensome towards AI infrastructure development . Two, data 
center operators  generally underwrite projects only when they have a high 
degree of confidence in securing power and, where necessary, may already be 
embedding the cost of upgrading energy infrastructure into their investment 
plans. We expect developers to be capable of passing on sustained increases in 
power infrastructure costs to their customers , which, for many 100  MW+ 
developments, are the major investment-grade hyperscalers.

### Page 6

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 6

Though data center development is receiving increasing national attention, it is 
still largely a local issue, and the most consequential elections will be at the state 
and local levels. We have seen a number of state -driven actions on data center s 
in recent months, including: 
• In July, New York State’s governor issued an executive order to establish 
a temporary moratorium on data center development  while the state 
develops a standardized framework of rules for key issues (including 
responsibilities for power and energy infrastructure upgrades; additional 
power generation; clean energy; water use, emission, and waste 
management; and state/local financ ial incentives). The New York 
legislature also passed a one -year moratorium on Department of 
Environmental Conservation permits and approvals for 20  MW+ data 
center developments. 
• In August, Texas’ governor directed the state’s public utility commission 
to conduct a comprehensive audit of every data center advancing 
through the ERCOT (Texas’s grid operator) interconnection process. The 
audit will cover several notable areas including public financial support, 
electricity demand and supply, water use, community impacts, and 
ownership and control. 
• In September, California’s governor passed a comprehensive regulatory 
package, covering electricity, water, reporting, and environmental 
review. Most of these provisions take effect January 1, 2027, and phase 
in through 2027 -2028, but the package does not ban data centers or 
impose a statewide moratorium on development activity. 
• Oregon, Pennsylvania, New Jersey, and other states have also 
established orders to create standards and frameworks governing data 
center development (but without imposing broad moratoriums). 
At the more local level, a large number of moratoriums have been passed on new 
data center development. Data collected by SemiAnalysis, an independent 
research firm, shows that, as of September, more than 300 local data  center 
moratoriums have been adopted nationwide across counties, municipalities, 
townships, and other local governments.  
However, these state and local moratoriums appear  thus far to be less impactful 
to near- and medium-term development activity than headline coverage might 
otherwise suggest. According to SemiAnalysis, only about 2.3 GW of the 38 GW 
of data center capacity planned or under construction for 2027 has faced delays 
as a result of  the New York state executive order (0.8 GW) and/or local 
moratoriums (1.5 GW). 
We believe the primary reasons that moratoriums are, in reality, less likely to 
impact near -term development activity are (1) that these moratoriums largely 
impact new projects and applications (rather than existing developments in  
progress), (2) there may be flexibility to relocate or rezone early-stage projects in 
response to moratoriums, and (3) data center developers are increasingly 
partnering with local communities to gain their support via investments and other 
programs (as we wrote about recently).  Potential regulatory risk has not yet 
slowed capex spending patterns, with capex spend still expected to climb from

### Page 7

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 7

$0.5 trillion in 2025 to $1.3 trillion by 2027; data centers typically represent ~20-
30% of the total bill of materials for new AI capacity.  
Although the practical impact on near- and medium-term development pipelines 
appears less pronounced than headlines might suggest, all this is not to say that 
data centers will not face increasing local opposition following the midterms. 
Indeed, i n our conversations with industry participants, there is an 
acknowledgment that greater public scrutiny of data centers is not going away.  
Additional moratoriums, zoning restrictions, permitting requirements, or utility -
related conditions could affect the pace, cost , and overall feasibility of future 
projects.
